SharedSymbolTableBucketSize 11–26 — 4 рабочий, uint. Average number of symbols per bucket in shared table
ShenandoahAdaptiveDecayFactor 17–26 — 0.5 экспериментальный, double. The decay factor (alpha) used for values in the weighted moving average of cycle time and allocation rate. Larger values give more weight to recent values.
ShenandoahAdaptiveInitialConfidence 17–26 — 1.8 экспериментальный, double. The number of standard deviations used to determine an initial margin of error for the average cycle time and average allocation rate. Increasing this value will cause the heuristic to initiate more concurrent cycles.
ShenandoahAdaptiveInitialSpikeThreshold 17–26 — 1.8 экспериментальный, double. If the most recently sampled allocation rate is more than this many standard deviations away from the moving average, then a cycle is initiated. This value controls how sensitive the heuristic is to allocation spikes. Decreasing this number increases the sensitivity.
ShenandoahAdaptiveSampleFrequencyHz 17–26 — 10 экспериментальный, uintx. The number of times per second to update the allocation rate moving average.
ShenandoahAdaptiveSampleSizeSeconds 17–26 — 10 экспериментальный, uintx. The size of the moving window over which the average allocation rate is maintained. The total number of samples is the product of this number and the sample frequency.
ShenandoahAgingCyclePeriod 25–26 — 1 экспериментальный, uintx. With generational mode, increment the age of objects andregions each time this many young-gen GC cycles are completed.
ShenandoahAllocationThreshold 11–26 — 0 экспериментальный, uintx. How many new allocations should happen since the last GC cycle before some heuristics trigger the collection. In percents of (soft) max heap size. Set to zero to effectively disable.
ShenandoahAllocFailureALot 11–26 — false диагностический, bool. Testing: make lots of artificial allocation failures.
ShenandoahAllocSpikeFactor 11–26 — 5 экспериментальный, uintx. How much of heap should some heuristics reserve for absorbing the allocation spikes. Larger value wastes more memory in non-emergency cases, but provides more safety in emergency cases. In percents of (soft) max heap size.
ShenandoahAllowOldMarkingPreemption 25–26 — true диагностический, bool. Allow young generation collections to suspend concurrent marking in the old generation.
ShenandoahAlwaysClearSoftRefs 11–26 — false экспериментальный, bool. Unconditionally clear soft references, instead of using any other cleanup policy. This minimizes footprint at expense ofmore soft reference churn in applications.
ShenandoahCardBarrier 25–26 — false диагностический, bool. Turn on/off card-marking post-write barrier in Shenandoah: true when ShenandoahGCMode is generational, false otherwise
ShenandoahCASBarrier 11–26 — true диагностический, bool. Turn on/off CAS barriers in Shenandoah
ShenandoahCloneBarrier 11–26 — true диагностический, bool. Turn on/off clone barriers in Shenandoah
ShenandoahCoalesceChance 25–26 — 0 диагностический, uintx. Testing: Abandon remaining mixed collections with this likelihood. Following each mixed collection, abandon all remaining mixed collection candidate regions with likelihood ShenandoahCoalesceChance. Abandoning a mixed collection will cause the old regions to be made parsable, rather than being evacuated.
ShenandoahCodeRootsStyle 11 — 2 диагностический, uintx. Use this style to scan the code cache roots: 0 - sequential iterator; 1 - parallel iterator; 2 - parallel iterator with cset filters;
ShenandoahControlIntervalAdjustPeriod 11–26 — 1000 экспериментальный, uintx. The time period for one step in control loop interval adjustment. Lower values make adjustments faster, at the expense of higher perf overhead. Time is in milliseconds.
ShenandoahControlIntervalMax 11–26 — 10 экспериментальный, uintx. The maximum sleep interval for control loop that drives the cycles. Lower values would increase GC responsiveness to changing heap conditions, at the expense of higher perf overhead. Time is in milliseconds.
ShenandoahControlIntervalMin 11–26 — 1 экспериментальный, uintx. The minimum sleep interval for the control loop that drives the cycles. Lower values would increase GC responsiveness to changing heap conditions, at the expense of higher perf overhead. Time is in milliseconds.
ShenandoahCriticalFreeThreshold 11–26 — 1 экспериментальный, uintx. How much of the heap needs to be free after recovery cycles, either Degenerated or Full GC to be claimed successful. If this much space is not available, next recovery step would be triggered.
ShenandoahDegeneratedGC 11–26 — true диагностический, bool. Enable Degenerated GC as the graceful degradation step. Disabling this option leads to degradation to Full GC instead. When running in passive mode, this can be toggled to measure either Degenerated GC or Full GC costs.
ShenandoahDoNotIgnoreGrowthAfterYoungCycles 25–26 — 50 экспериментальный, uintx. (Generational mode only) Even if the usage of old generation is below ShenandoahIgnoreOldGrowthBelowPercentage, trigger an old-generation mark if old has grown and this many consecutive young-gen collections have been completed following the preceding old-gen collection.
ShenandoahElasticTLAB 11–17 — true диагностический, bool. Use Elastic TLABs with Shenandoah
ShenandoahEvacReserve 11–26 — 5 экспериментальный, uintx. How much of (young-generation) heap to reserve for (young-generation) evacuations. Larger values allow GC to evacuate more live objects on every cycle, while leaving less headroom for application to allocate while GC is evacuating and updating references. This parameter is consulted at the end of marking, before selecting the collection set. If available memory at this time is smaller than the indicated reserve, the bound on collection set size is adjusted downward. The size of a generational mixed evacuation collection set (comprised of both young and old regions) is also bounded by this parameter. In percents of total (young-generation) heap size.
ShenandoahEvacReserveOverflow 11–26 — true экспериментальный, bool. Allow evacuations to overflow the reserved space. Enabling it will make evacuations more resilient when evacuation reserve/waste is incorrect, at the risk that application runs out of memory too early.
ShenandoahEvacTracking 25–26 — false* диагностический, bool. —
ShenandoahEvacWaste 11–26 — 1.2 экспериментальный, double. How much waste evacuations produce within the reserved space. Larger values make evacuations more resilient against evacuation conflicts, at expense of evacuating less on each GC cycle. Smaller values increase the risk of evacuation failures, which will trigger stop-the-world Full GC passes.
ShenandoahExpediteMixedThreshold 25–26 — 10 экспериментальный, uintx. When there are this many old regions waiting to be collected, trigger a mixed collection immediately.
ShenandoahExpeditePromotionsThreshold 25–26 — 5 экспериментальный, uintx. When Shenandoah expects to promote at least this percentage of the young generation, trigger a young collection to expedite these promotions.
ShenandoahFullGCThreshold 11–26 — 3 экспериментальный, uintx. How many back-to-back Degenerated GCs should happen before going to a Full GC.
ShenandoahGarbageThreshold 11–26 — 25 экспериментальный, uintx. How much garbage a region has to contain before it would be taken for collection. This a guideline only, as GC heuristics may select the region for collection even if it has little garbage. This also affects how much internal fragmentation the collector accepts. In percents of heap region size.
ShenandoahGCHeuristics 11–26 — adaptive рабочий, ccstr. GC heuristics to use. This fine-tunes the GC mode selected, by choosing when to start the GC, how much to process on each cycle, and what other features to automatically enable. Possible values are: adaptive - adapt to maintain the given amount of free heap at all times, even during the GC cycle; static - trigger GC when free heap falls below the threshold; aggressive - run GC continuously, try to evacuate everything; compact - run GC more frequently and with deeper targets to free up more memory.
ShenandoahGCMode 11–26 — satb рабочий, ccstr. GC mode to use. Among other things, this defines which barriers are in in use. Possible values are: satb - snapshot-at-the-beginning concurrent GC (three pass mark-evac-update); passive - stop the world GC only (either degenerated or full); generational - generational concurrent GC
ShenandoahGenerationalAdaptiveTenuring 26 — true экспериментальный, bool. (Generational mode only) Dynamically adapt tenuring age.
ShenandoahGenerationalCensusIgnoreOlderCohorts 25–26 — true экспериментальный, bool. (Generational mode only) Ignore mortality rates older than the oldest cohort under the tenuring age for the last cycle.
ShenandoahGenerationalHumongousReserve 25–26 — 0 экспериментальный, uintx. (Generational mode only) What percent of the heap should be reserved for humongous objects if possible. Old-generation collections will endeavor to evacuate old-gen regions within this reserved area even if these regions do not contain high percentage of garbage. Setting a larger value will cause more frequent old-gen collections. A smaller value will increase the likelihood that humongous object allocations fail, resulting in stop-the-world full GCs.
ShenandoahGenerationalMaxTenuringAge 25–26 — 15 экспериментальный, uintx. (Generational mode only) Ceiling for adaptive tenuring age. Setting floor and ceiling to the same value fixes the tenuring age; setting both to 1 simulates a poor approximation to AlwaysTenure, and setting both to 16 simulates NeverTenure.
ShenandoahGenerationalMinPIPUsage 25–26 — 30* экспериментальный, uintx. —
ShenandoahGenerationalMinTenuringAge 25–26 — 1 экспериментальный, uintx. (Generational mode only) Floor for adaptive tenuring age. Setting floor and ceiling to the same value fixes the tenuring age; setting both to 1 simulates a poor approximation to AlwaysTenure, and setting both to 16 simulates NeverTenure.
ShenandoahGenerationalTenuringCohortPopulationThreshold 25–26 — 4096* экспериментальный, size_t. (Generational mode only) Cohorts whose population is lower than this value in the previous census are ignored wrt tenuring decisions. Effectively this makes then tenurable as soon as all older cohorts are. Set this value to the largest cohort population volume that you are comfortable ignoring when making tenuring decisions.
ShenandoahGenerationalTenuringMortalityRateThreshold 25–26 — 0.1 экспериментальный, double. (Generational mode only) Cohort mortality rates below this value will be treated as indicative of longevity, leading to tenuring. A lower value delays tenuring, a higher value hastens it. Used only when ShenandoahGenerationalhenAdaptiveTenuring is enabled.
ShenandoahGuaranteedGCInterval 11–26 — 300000* экспериментальный, uintx. Many heuristics would guarantee a concurrent GC cycle at least with this interval. This is useful when large idle intervals are present, where GC can run without stealing time from active application. Time is in milliseconds. Setting this to 0 disables the feature.
ShenandoahGuaranteedOldGCInterval 25–26 — 600000* экспериментальный, uintx. Run a collection of the old generation at least this often. Heuristics may trigger collections more frequently. Time is in milliseconds. Setting this to 0 disables the feature.
ShenandoahGuaranteedYoungGCInterval 25–26 — 300000* экспериментальный, uintx. Run a collection of the young generation at least this often. Heuristics may trigger collections more frequently. Time is in milliseconds. Setting this to 0 disables the feature.
ShenandoahHumongousMoves 11–26 — true диагностический, bool. Allow moving humongous regions. This makes GC more resistant to external fragmentation that may otherwise fail other humongous allocations, at the expense of higher GC copying costs. Currently affects stop-the-world (Full) cycle only.
ShenandoahHumongousThreshold 11–21 — 100 экспериментальный, intx. Humongous objects are allocated in separate regions. This setting defines how large the object should be to be deemed humongous. Value is in percents of heap region size. This also caps the maximum TLAB size.
ShenandoahIgnoreGarbageThreshold 25–26 — 5 экспериментальный, uintx. When less than this amount of garbage (as a percentage of region size) exists within a region, the region will not be added to the collection set, even when the heuristic has chosen to aggressively add regions with less than ShenandoahGarbageThreshold amount of garbage into the collection set.
ShenandoahIgnoreOldGrowthBelowPercentage 25–26 — 10 экспериментальный, uintx. (Generational mode only) If the total usage of the old generation is smaller than this percent, we do not trigger old gen collections even if old has grown, except when ShenandoahGenerationalDoNotIgnoreGrowthAfterYoungCycles consecutive cycles have been completed following the preceding old-gen collection.
ShenandoahImmediateThreshold 11–26 — 70 экспериментальный, uintx. The cycle may shortcut when enough garbage can be reclaimed from the immediate garbage (completely garbage regions). In percents of total garbage found. Setting this threshold to 100 effectively disables the shortcut.
ShenandoahImplicitGCInvokesConcurrent 11–26 — false экспериментальный, bool. Should internally-caused GC requests invoke concurrent cycles, should they do the stop-the-world (Degenerated / Full GC)? Many heuristics automatically enable this. This option is similar to global ExplicitGCInvokesConcurrent.
ShenandoahInitFreeThreshold 11–26 — 70 экспериментальный, uintx. When less than this amount of memory is free within the heap or generation, trigger a learning cycle if we are in learning mode. Learning mode happens during initialization and following a drastic state change, such as following a degenerated or Full GC cycle. In percents of soft max heap size.
ShenandoahIUBarrier 11–21 — false диагностический, bool. Turn on/off I-U barriers barriers in Shenandoah
ShenandoahLearningSteps 11–26 — 5 экспериментальный, uintx. The number of cycles some heuristics take to collect in order to learn application and GC performance.
ShenandoahLoadRefBarrier 11–26 — true диагностический, bool. Turn on/off load-reference barriers in Shenandoah
ShenandoahLoopOptsAfterExpansion 11–17 — true диагностический, bool. Attempt more loop opts after barrier expansion.
ShenandoahMarkLoopStride 11–26 — 1000 экспериментальный, uintx. How many items to process during one marking iteration before checking for cancellation, yielding, etc. Larger values improve marking performance at expense of responsiveness.
ShenandoahMarkScanPrefetch 11–26 — 32 экспериментальный, intx. How many objects to prefetch ahead when traversing mark bitmaps.Set to 0 to disable prefetching.
ShenandoahMaxRegionSize 11–26 — 33554432* экспериментальный, size_t. With automatic region sizing, the regions would be at most this large.
ShenandoahMaxSATBBufferFlushes 11–26 — 5 экспериментальный, uintx. How many times to maximum attempt to flush SATB buffers at the end of concurrent marking.
ShenandoahMaxYoungPercentage 25–26 — 100 экспериментальный, uintx. The maximum percentage of the heap to use for the young generation. Heuristics will not adjust the young generation to be more than this.
ShenandoahMinFreeThreshold 11–26 — 10 экспериментальный, uintx. Percentage of free heap memory (or young generation, in generational mode) below which most heuristics trigger collection independent of other triggers. Provides a safety margin for many heuristics. In percents of (soft) max heap size.
ShenandoahMinimumOldTimeMs 25–26 — 100 экспериментальный, uintx. Minimum amount of time in milliseconds to run old collections before a young collection is allowed to run. This is intended to prevent starvation of the old collector. Setting this to 0 will allow back to back young collections to run during old collections.
ShenandoahMinOldGenGrowthPercent 25–26 — 12.5 экспериментальный, double. (Generational mode only) If the usage within old generation has grown by at least this percent of its live memory size at completion of the most recent old-generation marking effort, heuristics may trigger the start of a new old-gen collection.
ShenandoahMinRegionSize 11–26 — 262144* экспериментальный, size_t. With automatic region sizing, the regions would be at least this large.
ShenandoahMinYoungPercentage 25–26 — 20 экспериментальный, uintx. The minimum percentage of the heap to use for the young generation. Heuristics will not adjust the young generation to be less than this.
ShenandoahNMethodBarrier 17 — true диагностический, bool. Turn on/off NMethod entry barriers in Shenandoah
ShenandoahNoProgressThreshold 25–26 — 5 экспериментальный, uintx. After this number of consecutive Full GCs fail to make progress, Shenandoah will raise out of memory errors. Note that progress is determined by ShenandoahCriticalFreeThreshold
ShenandoahOldCompactionReserve 25–26 — 8 экспериментальный, uintx. During generational GC, prevent promotions from filling this number of heap regions. These regions are reserved for the purpose of supporting compaction of old-gen memory. Otherwise, old-gen memory cannot be compacted.
ShenandoahOldEvacRatioPercent 25–26 — 75 экспериментальный, uintx. The maximum proportion of evacuation from old-gen memory, expressed as a percentage. The default value 75 denotes that nomore than 75% of the collection set evacuation workload may be towards evacuation of old-gen heap regions. This limits both thepromotion of aged regions and the compaction of existing old regions. A value of 75 denotes that the total evacuation workmay increase to up to four times the young gen evacuation work.A larger value allows quicker promotion and allowsa smaller number of mixed evacuations to process the entire list of old-gen collection candidates at the cost of an increased disruption of the normal cadence of young-gen collections. A value of 100 allows a mixed evacuation to focus entirely on old-gen memory, allowing no young-gen regions to be collected, likely resulting in subsequent allocation failures because the allocation pool is not replenished. A value of 0 allows a mixed evacuation tofocus entirely on young-gen memory, allowing no old-gen regions to be collected, likely resulting in subsequent promotion failures and triggering of stop-the-world full GC events.
ShenandoahOldEvacWaste 25–26 — 1.4 экспериментальный, double. How much waste evacuations produce within the reserved space. Larger values make evacuations more resilient against evacuation conflicts, at expense of evacuating less on each GC cycle. Smaller values increase the risk of evacuation failures, which will trigger stop-the-world Full GC passes.
ShenandoahOldGarbageThreshold 25–26 — 15 экспериментальный, uintx. How much garbage an old region has to contain before it would be taken for collection.
ShenandoahOOMDuringEvacALot 11–26 — false диагностический, bool. Testing: simulate OOM during evacuation.
ShenandoahOptimizeStaticFinals 11 — true диагностический, bool. Optimize barriers on static final fields. Turn it off for maximum compatibility with reflection or JNI code that manipulates final fields.
ShenandoahPacing 11–25 устарел в 25, игнорируется с 26, удалён в 27 true экспериментальный, bool. Pace application allocations to give GC chance to start and complete before allocation failure is reached.
ShenandoahPacingCycleSlack 11–25 — 10 экспериментальный, uintx. How much of free space to take as non-taxable allocations the GC cycle. Larger value makes the pacing milder at the beginning of the GC cycle. Lower value makes the pacing less uniform during the cycle. In percent of free space.
ShenandoahPacingIdleSlack 11–25 — 2 экспериментальный, uintx. How much of heap counted as non-taxable allocations during idle phases. Larger value makes the pacing milder when collector is idle, requiring less rendezvous with control thread. Lower value makes the pacing control less responsive to out-of-cycle allocs. In percent of total heap size.
ShenandoahPacingMaxDelay 11–25 — 10 экспериментальный, uintx. Max delay for pacing application allocations. Larger values provide more resilience against out of memory, at expense at hiding the GC latencies in the allocation path. Time is in milliseconds. Setting it to arbitrarily large value makes GC effectively stall the threads indefinitely instead of going to degenerated or Full GC.
ShenandoahPacingSurcharge 11–25 — 1.1 экспериментальный, double. Additional pacing tax surcharge to help unclutter the heap. Larger values makes the pacing more aggressive. Lower values risk GC cycles finish with less memory than were available at the beginning of it.
ShenandoahParallelRegionStride 11–26 — 0 экспериментальный, uintx. How many regions to process at once during parallel region iteration. Affects heaps with lots of regions. Set to 0 to let Shenandoah to decide the best value.
ShenandoahPreclean 11 — true диагностический, bool. Do concurrent preclean phase before final mark: process definitely alive references to avoid dealing with them during pause.
ShenandoahPromoEvacWaste 25–26 — 1.2 экспериментальный, double. How much waste promotions produce within the reserved space. Larger values make evacuations more resilient against evacuation conflicts, at expense of promoting less on each GC cycle. Smaller values increase the risk of evacuation failures, which will trigger stop-the-world Full GC passes.
ShenandoahRefProcFrequency 11 — 5 экспериментальный, uintx. Process process weak (soft, phantom, finalizers) references every Nth cycle. Normally affects concurrent GC cycles only, as degenerated and full GCs would try to process references regardless. Set to zero to disable reference processing completely.
ShenandoahRegionSampling 11–26 — false экспериментальный, bool. Provide heap region sampling data via jvmstat.
ShenandoahRegionSamplingRate 11–26 — 40 экспериментальный, int. Sampling rate for heap region sampling. In milliseconds between the samples. Higher values provide more fidelity, at expense of more sampling overhead.
ShenandoahRegionSize 11–26 — 0 экспериментальный, size_t. Static heap region size. Set zero to enable automatic sizing.
ShenandoahSATBBarrier 11–26 — true диагностический, bool. Turn on/off SATB barriers in Shenandoah
ShenandoahSATBBufferSize 11–26 — 1024* экспериментальный, size_t. Number of entries in an SATB log buffer.
ShenandoahSelfFixing 11–17 — true диагностический, bool. Fix references with load reference barrier. Disabling this might degrade performance.
ShenandoahSoftMaxHeapSize 11 — 0 управляемый, size_t. Soft limit for maximum heap size (in bytes)
ShenandoahStackWatermarkBarrier 17–26 — true диагностический, bool. Turn on/off stack watermark barriers in Shenandoah
ShenandoahSuspendibleWorkers 11–17 — true экспериментальный, bool. Suspend concurrent GC worker threads at safepoints
ShenandoahTargetNumRegions 11–26 — 2048 экспериментальный, size_t. With automatic region sizing, this is the approximate number of regions that would be used, within min/max region size limits.
ShenandoahUncommit 11–26 — true экспериментальный, bool. Allow to uncommit memory under unused regions and metadata. This optimizes footprint at expense of allocation latency in regions that require committing back. Uncommits would be disabled by some heuristics, or with static heap size.
ShenandoahUncommitDelay 11–26 — 300000* экспериментальный, uintx. Uncommit memory for regions that were not used for more than this time. First use after that would incur allocation stalls. Actively used regions would never be uncommitted, because they do not become unused longer than this delay. Time is in milliseconds. Setting this delay to 0 effectively uncommits regions almost immediately after they become unused.
ShenandoahUnloadClassesFrequency 11–17 — 1 экспериментальный, uintx. Unload the classes every Nth cycle. Normally affects concurrent GC cycles, as degenerated and full GCs would try to unload classes regardless. Set to zero to disable class unloading.
ShenandoahVerify 11–26 — false диагностический, bool. Enable internal verification. This would catch many GC bugs, but it would also stall the collector during the verification, which prolongs the pauses and might hide other bugs.
ShenandoahVerifyLevel 11–26 — 4 диагностический, intx. Verification level, higher levels check more, taking more time. Accepted values are: 0 = basic heap checks; 1 = previous level, plus basic region checks; 2 = previous level, plus all roots; 3 = previous level, plus all reachable objects; 4 = previous level, plus all marked objects

ThreadPriorityPolicy 8–26 — 0 рабочий, int. 0 : Normal. VM chooses priorities that are appropriate for normal applications. On Windows applications are allowed to use higher native priorities. However, with ThreadPriorityPolicy=0, VM will not use the highest possible native priority, THREAD_PRIORITY_TIME_CRITICAL, as it may interfere with system threads. On Linux thread priorities are ignored because the OS does not support static priority in SCHED_OTHER scheduling class which is the only choice for non-root, non-realtime applications. 1 : Aggressive. Java thread priorities map over to the entire range of native thread priorities. Higher Java thread priorities map to higher native thread priorities. This policy should be used with care, as sometimes it can cause performance degradation in the application and/or the entire system. On Linux/BSD/macOS this policy requires root privilege or an extended capability.

VerifyBeforeIteration 8–26 — false диагностический, bool. Verify memory system before JVMTI iteration
VerifyConstraintCasts 25–26 — 0 диагностический, uint. Perform runtime checks to verify the value of a ConstraintCast lies inside its type0 = does not perform any verification, 1 = perform verification on ConstraintCastNodes that are present during code emission, 2 = Do not do widening of ConstraintCastNodes so that we can have more verification coverage
VerifyDuringGC 8–26 — false диагностический, bool. Verify memory system during GC (between phases)
VerifyDuringStartup 8–26 — false диагностический, bool. Verify memory system before executing any Java code during VM initialization
VerifyGCLevel 8–21 — 0 диагностический, intx. Generation level at which to start +VerifyBefore/AfterGC
VerifyGCStartAt 8–26 — 0 диагностический, uintx. GC invoke count where +VerifyBefore/AfterGC kicks in
VerifyGCType 11–26 — — диагностический, ccstrlist. GC type(s) to verify when Verify*GC is enabled.Available types are collector specific.
VerifyMergedCPBytecodes 8–11 игнорируется с 16, удалён в 17 true рабочий, bool. Verify bytecodes after RedefineClasses constant pool merging
VerifyMethodHandles 8–26 — false диагностический, bool. perform extra checks when constructing method handles
VerifyObjectStartArray 8–21 — true диагностический, bool. Verify GC object start array if verify before/after
VerifyReceiverTypes 17–26 — false диагностический, bool. Verify receiver types at runtime
VerifyRememberedSets 8–21 — false диагностический, bool. Verify GC remembered sets
VerifySecondarySupers 21–26 — false диагностический, bool. Check that linear and hashed secondary lookups return the same result.
VerifySharedSpaces 8–26 — false рабочий, bool. Verify integrity of shared spaces
VerifySilently 8 — false диагностический, bool. Do not print the verification progress
VerifyStringTableAtExit 8–26 — false диагностический, bool. verify StringTable contents at exit
VerifySubSet 8–26 — — диагностический, ccstrlist. Memory sub-systems to verify when Verify*GC flag(s) are enabled. One or more sub-systems can be specified in a comma separated string. Sub-systems are: threads, heap, symbol_table, string_table, codecache, dictionary, classloader_data_graph, metaspace, jni_handles, codecache_oops, resolved_method_table, stringdedup
VMContinuations 21–26 — true* экспериментальный, bool. Enable VM continuations support
VMThreadHintNoPreempt 8–11 устарел в 11, игнорируется с 12, удалён в 13 false рабочий, bool. (Solaris only) Give VM thread an extra quanta
VMThreadPriority 8–26 — -1 рабочий, int. The native priority at which the VM thread should run (-1 means no change)
VMThreadStackSize 8–26 — 0* рабочий, intx. Non-Java Thread Stack Size (in Kbytes)

WhiteBoxAPI 8–26 — false диагностический, bool. Enable internal testing APIs
WorkAroundNPTLTimedWaitHang 8 — 1 рабочий, intx. (Unstable, Linux-specific) avoid NPTL-FUTEX hang pthread_cond_timedwait
WorkStealingHardSpins 8–26 — 4096 экспериментальный, uintx. Number of iterations in a spin loop between checks on time out of hard spin
WorkStealingSleepMillis 8–26 — 1 экспериментальный, uintx. Sleep time when sleep is used for yields
WorkStealingSpinToYieldRatio 8–26 — 10 экспериментальный, uintx. Ratio of hard spins to calls to yield
WorkStealingYieldsBeforeSleep 8–26 — 5000 экспериментальный, uintx. Number of yields before a sleep is done during work stealing

Таблица I.22. Флаги HotSpot на букву W.

Флаги на букву X

Флаг JDK Снятие По умолчанию Описание
X86ICacheSync 25–26 — -1 диагностический, int. Select the X86 ICache sync mechanism: -1 = auto-select; 0 = none (dangerous); 1 = CLFLUSH loop; 2 = CLFLUSHOPT loop; 3 = CLWB loop; 4 = single CPUID; 5 = single SERIALIZE. Explicitly selected mechanism will fail at startup if hardware does not support it.

← Назад [Приложение H. Собеседование об опыте: как рассказывать о проектах

Источники

  • Oracle. The java Command, JDK 25 — первичный; виды опций, синтаксис и единицы, описания стандартных, дополнительных и расширенных опций, устаревшие, игнорируемые и удалённые опции. Для снятых флагов — те же страницы JDK 17 и JDK 8. Проверено: 2026-11-17.
  • OpenJDK, исходники HotSpot — первичный; определения флагов (globals.hpp и файлы *_globals.hpp сборщиков и компиляторов) с описанием, значением по умолчанию и диапазоном, таблица снятия флагов special_jvm_flags в arguments.cpp; теги jdk-9…jdk-26-ga, ветки jdk8u, jdk11u, jdk17u, jdk21u. Проверено: 2026-11-17.
  • Страницы JEP на openjdk.org/jeps, номера которых указаны в статьях, — первичный; версии JDK, в которых появились и были сняты сборщики и режимы.
  • Стенд labs/jvm-options — собственные запуски: флаги шести версий JDK под Windows (flags.sh) и Linux (flags-linux.sh), таблица флагов (catalog.py → catalog.tsv), стадии снятия (lifecycle.py → lifecycle.tsv), поведение JVM на устаревших и удалённых флагах (lifecycle-demo.sh), порядок применения опций (order.sh), -Xverify:none и журнал GC (xverify.txt, gc-logging.txt), упоминания опций в книге (mentions.py). Выполнено: 2026-11-17.

  1. Oracle. The java Command, JDK 25, разделы «Overview of Java Options», «Standard Options for Java», «Extra Options for Java», «Advanced … Options» и «Using the JDK_JAVA_OPTIONS Launcher Environment Variable»; для опций, которых в JDK 25 уже нет, — те же разделы страниц JDK 17 и JDK 8. ↩↩↩↩↩

  2. Стенд labs/jvm-options: flags.sh и flags-linux.sh запускают java -XX:+UnlockDiagnosticVMOptions -XX:+UnlockExperimentalVMOptions -XX:+PrintFlagsFinal -version на каждой сборке (выводы flags-<версия>.txt и flags-linux-<версия>.txt); catalog.py сводит их с определениями флагов из исходников HotSpot (src/*.hpp) в catalog.tsv; lifecycle.py собирает стадии снятия из arguments.cpp JDK 8u и 9–26 в lifecycle.tsv. ↩↩↩↩

  3. Стенд labs/jvm-options: скрипт order.sh, вывод order.txt; JDK 25.0.5, итоговое значение MaxHeapSize по -XX:+PrintFlagsFinal. ↩

  4. Стенд labs/jvm-options: скрипт lifecycle-demo.sh, вывод lifecycle-demo.txt; сборки Temurin 11.0.29, 17.0.21, 21.0.13, 25.0.5 и 26.0.2.1. Предупреждение JDK 17 в листинге разбито на две строки для ширины страницы. ↩
